Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on operators structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 32. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 500 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 32 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the operators structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 500. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
